# Global Tokens

> Route: `/tokens`

# Global Tokens

Browse global design variables and use the package-root API to read, update and subscribe to the current page's styles.

## Quick start

Every name starts with --. Values are CSS strings for colors, lengths, fonts and durations. getToken/getTokens return browser-cascaded values; GLOBAL\_TOKENS stores source expressions, dark overrides and font-tier overrides.

tokens.tsx

```tsx
import {
  getToken,
  getTokens,
  setToken,
  setTokens,
  resetTokens,
  subscribeTokens,
  useTokens,
  GLOBAL_TOKENS,
} from "@hustradio/unitary-ui";
import "@hustradio/unitary-ui/styles.css";

const radius = getToken("--radius");
const motion = getTokens(["--uni-duration-fast", "--ease-snap"]);
setToken("--radius", "8px");
setTokens({ "--primary": "#0070f3", "--primary-foreground": "#ffffff" });
const source = GLOBAL_TOKENS["--radius"].value;
const unsubscribe = subscribeTokens(() => console.log(getToken("--radius")));
unsubscribe();
resetTokens(["--radius", "--primary", "--primary-foreground"]);

function RadiusValue() {
  const values = useTokens(["--radius"]);
  return <output>{values["--radius"]}</output>;
}
```

## Live editor

The editor starts with the preview scope. Choosing the entire page writes root overrides until reset or reload; switching themes retains inline overrides.

Show code

TokensExample.tsx

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Select, SelectItem } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { useI18n } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { setTokens, resetTokens, useTokens, type TokenTarget } from "@/lib/tokens";

const names = ["--primary", "--primary-foreground", "--radius", "--uni-duration-fast"] as const;

export function TokensExample() {
  const { locale } = useI18n();
  const zh = locale === "zh-CN";
  const [scope, setScope] = useState("local");
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<TokenTarget | null>(null);
  const options = { target: scope === "global" ? undefined : target };
  const values = useTokens(names, options);
  return (
    <div data-token-editor className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <Select
          label={zh ? "作用范围" : "Scope"}
          value={scope}
          onValueChange={setScope}
          layout={{ width: 180 }}
        >
          <SelectItem value="local">{zh ? "仅演示区域" : "Demo only"}</SelectItem>
          <SelectItem value="global">{zh ? "整个页面" : "Entire page"}</SelectItem>
        </Select>
        <Button
          typeName="button"
          onClick={() =>
            setTokens({ "--primary": "#0070f3", "--primary-foreground": "#ffffff" }, options)
          }
        >
          {zh ? "蓝色" : "Blue"}
        </Button>
        <Button
          typeName="button"
          onClick={() =>
            setTokens({ "--primary": "#0b7a53", "--primary-foreground": "#ffffff" }, options)
          }
        >
          {zh ? "绿色" : "Green"}
        </Button>
        <Button typeName="button" type="secondary" onClick={() => resetTokens(names, options)}>
          {zh ? "恢复当前主题" : "Reset to theme"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
        <Select
          label={zh ? "圆角" : "Radius"}
          value={["0px", "8px", "16px"].includes(values["--radius"]) ? values["--radius"] : ""}
          placeholder={zh ? "选择圆角" : "Choose radius"}
          onValueChange={(value) => setTokens({ "--radius": value }, options)}
          layout={{ width: 180 }}
        >
          {["0px", "8px", "16px"].map((value) => (
            <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>
              {value}
            </SelectItem>
          ))}
        </Select>
        <Select
          label={zh ? "反馈动画时长" : "Feedback duration"}
          value={
            ["100ms", "250ms", "500ms"].includes(values["--uni-duration-fast"])
              ? values["--uni-duration-fast"]
              : ""
          }
          placeholder={zh ? "选择时长" : "Choose duration"}
          onValueChange={(value) => setTokens({ "--uni-duration-fast": value }, options)}
          layout={{ width: 180 }}
        >
          {["100ms", "250ms", "500ms"].map((value) => (
            <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>
              {value}
            </SelectItem>
          ))}
        </Select>
      </div>
      <div
        ref={setTarget}
        data-token-preview
        className="rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-5 text-foreground"
      >
        <p className="mb-3 text-copy-14">
          {zh
            ? "悬停或聚焦按钮，观察颜色、圆角和动画。"
            : "Hover or focus the button to inspect its color, radius and animation."}
        </p>
        <Button typeName="button" className="rounded-md transition-transform hover:scale-105">
          {zh ? "预览按钮" : "Preview button"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <dl data-token-values className="grid min-w-0 gap-2 text-copy-13-mono">
        {names.map((name) => (
          <div
            key={name}
            className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-x-4 rounded-md bg-surface px-3 py-2"
          >
            <dt className="break-all">{name}</dt>
            <dd className="break-all" data-token-value={name}>
              {values[name] || "—"}
            </dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Scope and reset

TokenOptions.target defaults to document.documentElement. Pass an element for local overrides; explicit null represents an unmounted target, with empty reads and false writes. TokenWriteOptions.priority accepts "" or "important". Reset removes selected inline token values, preserves other styles and restores the current cascade and theme. The API does not persist changes.

scoped-tokens.ts

```tsx
const target = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>("#preview");
setTokens({ "--radius": "16px", "--primary": "#0b7a53" }, { target });
const current = getTokens(["--radius", "--primary"], { target });
resetTokens(["--radius", "--primary"], { target });
```

Tokens follow browser cascade rules. Inherited aliases may already be resolved on an ancestor. For a local theme, override the semantic variable consumed by the component (such as --primary); changing a palette variable does not necessarily re-resolve inherited aliases.

## API

<table class="w-full min-w-[32rem]" aria-label="Token API"><thead><tr><th class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Function</th><th class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Returns</th><th class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Behavior</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>getToken(name, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>string</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Read a token's current cascaded value.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>getTokens(names?, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>TokenValues</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Read selected tokens; defaults to all runtime tokens.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>setToken(name, value, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>boolean</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Write one runtime token on the target element.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>setTokens(values, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>boolean</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Validate all entries before writing the batch.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>resetToken(name, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>boolean</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Remove one inline override.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>resetTokens(names?, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>boolean</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Remove selected overrides; defaults to every runtime token.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>subscribeTokens(listener, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>() =&gt; void</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">Subscribe to changes; returns an unsubscribe function.</td></tr><tr><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>useTokens(names?, options?)</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13"><code>TokenValues</code></td><td class="border-b border-border p-3 text-left align-top text-copy-13">React subscription with a cached, readonly snapshot.</td></tr></tbody></table>

TokenName covers the whole catalog; RuntimeTokenName covers writable variables; TokenGroup is the group union. TokenTarget accepts HTMLElement or SVGElement. TokenValues is a readonly name-to-string map. TOKEN\_NAMES and RUNTIME\_TOKEN\_NAMES provide enumeration lists.

Unknown names, build mappings, empty values or invalid priority throw TypeError. A CSS string is not guaranteed to be valid for every consuming property. Subscriptions respond to API writes, target/ancestor class/style/data-theme/data-font-tier changes, resize, and color-scheme/reduced-motion media changes. Read explicitly after direct CSSStyleSheet edits or asynchronous stylesheet loading. SSR reads are empty and writes return false; useTokens hydrates from an empty server snapshot and updates after mounting.

## Variables and build mappings

Runtime entries can be changed with setToken. Build mappings originate in @theme inline, whose expressions may be embedded in utilities. Changing the mapping variable may not affect existing classes, so writes are rejected; edit the runtime variable referenced by its value. For example, --radius affects rounded-sm/rounded-md, while rounded-lg has an independent fixed mapping. Breakpoints build media queries and cannot change responsive breakpoints through the runtime API.

\--font-metric-cjk-ratio, --font-metric-digit-w, --font-metric-baseline and --font-metric-colon are measured outputs written by applyFontMetrics rather than static catalog entries. The font probe recalculates them. Font-tier overrides appear in variants.

505 entries generated from the stylesheet, including 415 runtime variables. The table retains source var()/calc() expressions; dark mode inherits the base value when no override exists. Run pnpm run tokens:generate then pnpm run tokens:check after changing definitions.
